# Private evidence identity index

Raw screenshots, device metadata, APKs, logs, temporary tests/scripts and owner files are **not published**. Local artifacts remain under ignored `.audit/`; this table permits identity/hash correlation only. A local path is not a downloadable GitHub proof. Rejected evidence is retained and excluded from accepted coverage.

2168registered paths:1950accepted /1824unique accepted image hashes;218rejected paths;111bounded physical captures. Images and semantic state counts are distinct. Source baseline: `8157dcc275f1349f299e411c611888daafc035a0`.
